### [103] [Revisiting the Coupling of Thermodynamics and Electromagnetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07595)
*Stefanie Braun,Henning Struchtrup,Manuel Torrilhon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了运动介质中连续介质热力学与电磁理论的耦合，通过两条路径——Dreyer等人的公理化唯象理论和Mazur的统计力学方法——进行推导和比较，揭示了两者在结构上的一致性，并澄清了电动力强度、洛伦兹磁化以及熵函数不对称性等关键概念的本质来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动介质中可极化、可磁化物质的连续介质热力学与电磁理论的耦合存在理论分歧，需厘清不同推导路径（公理化与统计力学）之间的一致性与差异，并解决关键量的来源与符号问题。

Method: 两条路径：路径一为Dreyer、Guhlke和Müller的公理化体理论，基于普适平衡律和熵原理封闭方程组；路径二为Mazur的统计力学方法，通过对含内载流子的原子系综平均得到宏观麦克斯韦方程。作者补充推导了Mazur未完成的守恒律，并估算了质量修正项的量级，最后对两条路径的结果进行结构比较。

Result: （1）内能平衡的源项必须用非对流电流构建，极化电流和洛伦兹磁化通过一个恒等式进入，该恒等式同时确定了容许的熵变量和束缚电流假设的符号；（2）经极化与磁化的重新定义后，两套方程在结构上一致；（3）唯一的不可约差异来自微观场涨落的动量贡献，纯宏观理论无法再现；（4）电动力强度E和洛伦兹磁化M非人为建模选择，而是自然涌现；（5）熵函数的不对称外观是所选能量变量的结果，非理论缺陷。

Conclusion: 公理化唯象路径与统计力学路径在电磁-热力学耦合问题上达到结构一致，仅微观涨落动量项不可约。关键量如电动力强度与洛伦兹磁化具有内在必然性，熵函数不对称性源于能量变量选取，澄清了理论中的若干基本争议。

Abstract: We revisit the coupling of continuum thermodynamics and electromagnetic theory for polarisable and magnetisable matter in motion. Two routes are followed and then compared. The first route is the axiomatic bulk theory of Dreyer, Guhlke and Müller, in which universal balance laws are closed by an entropy principle. We show that the source of the internal energy balance must be built with the non-convective electric current, that the polarisation current and the Lorentz magnetisation enter through one single identity, which Dreyer et al.\ do not write down, and that this identity fixes both the admissible entropy variables and the signs of the bound-current ansatz. The second route is the statistical-mechanical one of Mazur, in which the macroscopic Maxwell equations are obtained by ensemble averaging over a system of atoms with internal charge carriers. Mazur stops before the conservation laws, so we derive them, and we estimate the size of the mass-correction terms that appear. The comparison shows that after a redefinition of polarisation and magnetisation the two sets of equations agree structurally. The only irreducible difference is a momentum contribution from microscopic field fluctuations, which can not be reproduced in a purely macroscopic theory. We further show that the electromotive intensity $\mathcal{E}$ and the Lorentz magnetisation $\mathcal{M}$ are not modelling choices but appear by themselves, and that the asymmetric look of the entropy function is a consequence of the chosen energy variable and not a defect of the theory.

</details>


### [104] [Hexagonal Stacking Maximizes Proton Configurational Entropy among Ice-I Polytypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07613)
*Zhengyue Chen,Sheng Ran*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过非负转移算子和Schatten-Hölder不等式，证明交替六方堆叠在冰规则计数上最大化构型常数；利用Nagle正偶子图展开和Finner超图Hölder不等式，将冰I的残熵w限定在1.503360至1.540196之间，其中立方冰Ic的上界为1.527699。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰I存在立方、六方及混合层堆叠结构，但严格的熵比较此前仅聚焦于两种理想端元。本文旨在对所有周期性均匀配位多型体进行统一的残熵比较，填补混合堆叠构型熵分析的空白。

Method: 1）用非负转移算子K及其转置表示每种循环均匀配位堆叠；2）对偶数长度词应用Schatten-Hölder不等式，证明交替六方堆叠最大化冰规则计数；3）利用Nagle正偶子图展开在精确枚举的不相交块上获得下界；4）通过Finner的二阶超图Hölder不等式和双副本棱柱转移算子的有理Collatz-Wielandt证书得到上界。

Result: 得出残熵范围1.503360 ≤ w ≤ 1.540196，其中立方冰Ic满足w(Ic) ≤ 1.527699。交替六方堆叠被证明在所有周期性均匀配位多型体中具有最大构型常数。

Conclusion: 本文建立了冰I所有周期性均匀配位堆叠的残熵统一理论框架，证明交替六方堆叠是最优构型，并显著收紧了残熵的上下界，为冰多型体的热力学稳定性排序提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: Ice I admits cubic, hexagonal, and mixed layer stackings, but rigorous entropy comparisons have focused on the two ideal endmembers. We represent every cyclic uniform-registry stacking by a word in a nonnegative transfer operator K and its transpose. For every such even-length word, applying the Schatten-Hölder inequality proves that alternating hexagonal stacking maximizes the ice-rule count at every common finite cross-section; the configuration constant is therefore maximal among all periodic uniform-registry polytypes. We obtain the lower endpoint by restricting Nagle's positive even-subgraph expansion to exactly enumerated disjoint blocks. Finner's degree-two hypergraph Hölder inequality and rational Collatz-Wielandt certificates for two-replica prism transfer operators give the upper endpoints. These constructions yield $1.503360 \le w \le 1.540196$, with $w(\mathrm{Ic}) \le 1.527699$.

</details>


### [105] [Fuzzy Spectroscopy of Bound States in Massive Quantum Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07655)
*Joseph Taylor,Matthew Yusuf,Zlatko Papić*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用基于非对易"模糊"几何的正规化方法，在模糊环面和球面上对伊辛量子场论进行非微扰谱学分析：在(1+1)维重现了磁扰动下的E₈介子质量谱，并通过改变环面纵横比连续追踪了从1D到2D维度渡越过程中第二轻E₈介子的演化，最终在2D模糊几何上获得了与胶球质量估计一致的标量态和阈值以上响应特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 禁闭量子场论中束缚态（如介子、胶球）的连续谱学在超越一维空间时面临严峻的非微扰挑战，迫切需要一种能够跨越维度、连续追踪束缚态演化的非微扰谱学方法。

Method: 采用基于非对易"模糊"几何（fuzzy geometry）的正规化方案，在模糊环面（fuzzy torus）和模糊球面（fuzzy sphere）上构建伊辛量子场论模型。通过在薄模糊环面上重现(1+1)D磁扰动伊辛QFT的E₈介子谱进行基准验证，随后增大环面纵横比实现从1D到2D的有效维度渡越，并结合淬火动力学（quench dynamics）探测远离平衡态的激发谱。

Result: 在薄模糊环面上成功重现了(1+1)D伊辛QFT的普适低能E₈介子质量谱；通过增大环面纵横比，连续追踪到第二轻E₈介子在维度渡越过程中的演化；在2D模糊环面和球面上发现了一个阈值以下的标量能级以及阈值以上的响应特征，与先前的胶球质量估计一致；淬火动力学同样揭示了相同的激发模式。

Conclusion: 模糊几何可作为大质量QFT的非微扰谱学探针，能够有效描述束缚态在维度渡越过程中的连续演化，为禁闭量子场论的谱学研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Mesons and glueballs are paradigmatic bound states of confining quantum field theories (QFTs), but their nonperturbative spectroscopy in the continuum remains challenging beyond one spatial dimension. Here we perform such spectroscopy for the Ising QFT using a recently developed regularization based on noncommutative ``fuzzy'' geometry. On a thin fuzzy torus, we reproduce the universal low-lying $\mathbb E_8$ meson masses of the magnetically perturbed $(1{+}1)\mathrm{D}$ Ising QFT. By increasing the torus aspect ratio, we continuously track the second-lightest $\mathbb E_8$ meson as the system effectively crosses over from 1D to 2D. On the 2D fuzzy torus and sphere, we find a subthreshold scalar level and above-threshold response features consistent with previous estimates of glueball masses. The same excitations are revealed away from equilibrium using quench dynamics. Our results establish fuzzy geometries as nonperturbative spectroscopic probes of massive QFTs, including their bound-state evolution through dimensional crossover.

</details>


### [106] [Classical fractons with cosmological fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07672)
*Akash Singh,Dileep P. Jatkar,S. L. Sondhi,Abhishodh Prakash*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有尺度不变性和偶极守恒的分形子哈密顿系统。通过将坐标分离为尺度和形状变量，系统展现出自主的形状动力学，其不动点决定膨胀粒子的分布，且尺度演化呈现幂律形式。特别地，参数(α,β)=(-2,1)的模型在尺度演化、不动点方程、大N分布及引力对偶等方面具有独特性质，可复现平坦物质主导宇宙学的关键结构，成为无需精细调节的宇宙学动力学玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典分形子系统在完整相空间中不存在吸引子，但投影到形状或构型空间后却可以产生吸引子行为。作者旨在探索这一机制能否自然地产生类似宇宙学的动力学特征，特别是寻找一个无需精细调节便能涌现出平坦物质主导宇宙学（如Einstein-de Sitter膨胀、Janus点、双向时间箭头等）结构的微观模型。

Method: 研究一个具有尺度不变性和偶极守恒的双参数分形子哈密顿族 H_{α,β}。通过将坐标分离为整体尺度 R 和形状变量 w，得到自主的形状动力学方程。分析形状不动点（即幂律Riesz势的中心构型），推导尺度演化的解析形式 R(t)∝|t|^{α/(α-β)}。对特殊参数(α,β)=(-2,1)进行重点分析，包括其不动点稳定性、大N数值模拟、簇结构形成以及有效不等质量分形子动力学的尺度分离猜想，并建立与零能量牛顿引力系统的对偶关系。

Result: （1）形状不动点导致纯尺度演化 R(t)∝|t|^{α/(α-β)}，不动点方程对应Riesz势的中心构型。（2）(α,β)=(-2,1)模型具有独特性：尺度演化为Einstein-de Sitter形式 R(t)∝|t|^{2/3}，不动点方程为等质量牛顿中心构型，大N分布为均匀球，存在零能量牛顿引力对偶。（3）不动点局部稳定，中等N模拟可从随机初始条件收敛至不动点。（4）大N模拟揭示更丰富的结构：束缚团簇保持近似固定物理尺寸和内部运动，团簇质心趋近不等质量牛顿中心构型并保持大尺度均匀性。（5）尺度分离猜想导出质心的有效不等质量分形子动力学及零能量牛顿引力对偶。（6）轨迹展示双向时间箭头：尺度和形状复杂度从Janus点向外增长，玻尔兹曼熵对数增长。

Conclusion: 在特殊的(α,β)=(-2,1)分形子模型中，所有宇宙学类比特征——Einstein-de Sitter膨胀、Janus点、双向时间箭头、熵增长——均作为吸引子性质涌现，无需精细调节初始条件。这使得该模型成为研究宇宙学动力学的理想微观玩具模型，为理解宇宙学中吸引子行为的微观起源提供了新视角。

Abstract: Classical fractons are Hamiltonian systems that can develop attractors after projection onto configuration or shape variables, although the full phase space admits none. We study a scale-invariant, dipole-conserving two-parameter family of fracton Hamiltonians $H_{α,β}$. By separating coordinates into scale and shape, we obtain autonomous shape dynamics that admit fixed points which leave a purely scale evolution of the form $R(t)\propto |t|^{α/(α-β)}$. The shape fixed points, which determine the distribution of the expanding particles, are central configurations of power-law Riesz potentials. The distinguished model $(α,β)=(-2,1)$ is unique: its scale evolution takes the Einstein-de Sitter form $R(t)\propto |t|^{2/3}$, its fixed-point equation is the equal-mass Newtonian central-configuration, its large-$N$ distribution is a homogeneous ball, and its homothetic trajectories admit a zero-energy Newtonian gravitational dual. The fixed points are locally stable, and simulations at moderate $N$ approach them from random initial data. Large $N$ simulations reveal a richer class of fixed-points: bound clusters of approximately fixed physical size retain internal motion, while their centers approach unequal-mass Newtonian central configurations and preserve large-scale homogeneity. A scale-separation conjecture yields an effective unequal-mass fracton dynamics for the centers and a corresponding zero-energy Newtonian gravitational dual. Trajectories generically exhibit a bidirectional arrow of time: scale and shape complexity grow away from a Janus point, while Boltzmann entropy grows logarithmically. Together, these features reproduce the salient structure of a flat matter-dominated cosmology. In the distinguished fracton model, all these cosmological analogues emerge as attractor properties, making it a toy model for cosmological dynamics without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [107] [Turning angle analysis reveals hidden anisotropies in the anomalous diffusion of molecules in live cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07975)
*Michał Balcerek,Adrian Pacheco-Pozo,Agnieszka Wyłomańska,Diego Krapf*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出利用转角统计量检测活细胞内各向异性反常扩散的新方法，基于二维各向异性分数布朗运动模型，在量子点和膜蛋白追踪数据中揭示了隐藏的空间各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活细胞内的分子运动轨迹通常短、噪声大且取向随机，传统分析方法无法有效检测空间各向异性，亟需一种能从不规则轨迹中提取各向异性信号的新策略。

Method: 采用二维各向异性分数布朗运动（fBM）模型，考虑随机取向，从理论上推导并模拟验证转角分布保留各向异性特征；将该方法应用于单粒子追踪数据，包括HeLa细胞胞质中的量子点以及海马神经元膜蛋白（Nav1.6通道和CD4糖蛋白）。

Result: 转角分布对模型施加的各向异性高度敏感，成功揭示量子点和Nav1.6通道运动中的隐藏各向异性，以及在CD4糖蛋白特定动力学状态下检测到各向异性。

Conclusion: 转角分析是探测复杂环境中分子运动组织的有效策略，各向异性反常扩散是细胞内动力学中被忽视的重要特征。

Abstract: Molecular motion within living cells provides a window into the physical principles underlying intracellular organization and transport. Yet a fundamental limitation remains: measured trajectories are often short, noisy, and randomly oriented, rendering spatial anisotropies inaccessible to conventional analyses. We show that turning-angle statistics provide a robust approach for uncovering anisotropies in anomalous diffusion. Using a two-dimensional anisotropic fractional Brownian motion model with random orientations, we show theoretically and through simulations that turning-angle distributions preserve signatures of anisotropy. We apply this approach to single-particle tracking data, including quantum dots in the cytoplasm of HeLa cells and membrane proteins in hippocampal neurons. Turning angles reveal hidden anisotropies in the motion of quantum dots and Nav 1.6 channels, and in specific dynamical states of glycoprotein CD4. These results establish turning-angle analysis as a powerful strategy for detecting organization in complex environments and reveal that anisotropic anomalous diffusion is an overlooked feature of intracellular dynamics.

</details>


### [108] [Low-frequency output fluctuations in an open exclusion process with particle pausing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08074)
*Quentin Thommen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个驱动晶格气体中缓慢内态对输运性质的影响。在开放TASEP模型中，粒子可在活跃与暂停态间可逆切换。研究发现，增加暂停率会平滑降低平均流，但长窗口Fano因子呈强非单调变化。最大Fano因子对应的平均暂停粒子数在不同系统尺寸下稳定在N_p≈1.5–2，而相应的暂停率按k_p^max ∝ L^{-1}标度。通过恒出生-线性死亡近似，集体 crossover 被转化为有限尺寸位移，给出独立暂停、强阻塞极限下的N_p*≈1.50。模拟表明暂停数呈过度离散，且在固定N_p下，较慢的去暂停速率同时增加关联时间和噪声振幅。停留时间和结构分析进一步分离了相关慢变量：无暂停/有暂停停留时间尺度跟踪输出关联时间，而噪声振幅则跟随最大粒子团簇的涨落而非其平均大小。因此，低频输出噪声揭示了一种由慢缺陷动力学与交通堵塞重组共同塑造的间歇性有限尺寸机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡系统中缓慢内态如何影响输运过程的均值通量及其涨落（噪声）仍未充分理解。本文旨在揭示开放式TASEP中粒子在活跃与暂停态间切换时，有限系统的平均电流和电流涨落（Fano因子）如何受控，特别是寻找噪声最大值对应的普适条件和标度行为。

Method: 采用有限开放边界条件下的 totally asymmetric simple exclusion process (TASEP)，引入粒子在活跃与暂停态的可逆切换。通过出口计数统计获得平均通量和长窗口Fano因子，改变暂停/去暂停速率进行数值模拟（晶格长度L=50–500）。构建恒出生-线性死亡的极小近似模型将集体 crossover 转化为有限尺寸位移，并结合停留时间分布和粒子团簇结构分析分离慢变量。

Result: 1) 增大的暂停率使平均电流平滑下降，而Fano因子呈强非单调性；2) 噪声峰值处平均暂停粒子数N_p在L=50–500范围内稳定在约1.5–2，对应暂停标度k_p^max ∝ L^{-1}；3) 恒出生-线性死亡近似给出独立暂停、强阻塞极限下N_p*≈1.50；4) 暂停数过度离散，固定N_p时较慢去暂停增加关联时间和噪声振幅；5) 停留时间分析将无暂停/有暂停尺度与输出关联时间关联，噪声振幅由最大团簇涨落而非其平均大小决定。

Conclusion: 低频输出噪声可作为识别有限尺寸间歇性机制的探针，该机制由慢缺陷动力学与交通堵塞重组共同决定。噪声峰值出现的条件具有简单的标度行为，可归约为一个阶一量级的集体 crossover，并通过最小近似模型预测。该框架为理解缓慢内态驱动的非平衡涨落提供了定量工具。

Abstract: Slow internal states reshape both the mean throughput and the temporal organization of a driven lattice gas. Exit-counting statistics reveal this effect in a finite open totally asymmetric simple exclusion process whose particles reversibly switch between active and paused states. Increasing pausing lowers the mean current smoothly, whereas the long-window Fano factor is strongly nonmonotonic. At the reference boundary rates, the maximum remains near a measured mean paused population \(N_p=Lρ_{\rm paused}\simeq1.5\)--\(2\) across lattice lengths L=50-500, while the corresponding pausing rate scales as \(k_p^{\rm max}\propto L^{-1}\). A minimal constant-birth, linear-death approximation translates an order-one collective crossover into this finite-size displacement and gives \(N_p^\star\simeq1.50\) in the independent-pause, strong-blocking limit. The simulations delimit this approximation: the pause number is overdispersed, and at fixed \(N_p\), slower unpausing increases both the correlation time and the noise amplitude. Residence-time and structural analyses further separate the relevant slow variables. The pause-free versus pause-containing residence-time scale tracks the fitted output-correlation time, whereas the noise amplitude follows fluctuations, rather than the mean size, of the largest particle cluster. Low-frequency output noise therefore identifies an intermittent finite-size regime shaped jointly by slow-defect kinetics and traffic-jam reorganization.

</details>


### [109] [Cubic-Equivariant Neural Density Functional Theory for Three-Dimensional Lattice Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08137)
*Jens Weimar,Martin Oettel,Alessandro Simon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种神经经典密度泛函，可直接作用于无约束的三维密度分布，以简单立方晶格上的平行硬立方体为测试平台，通过全卷积网络学习单粒子直接关联泛函，相比传统晶格基本测度泛函显著提升了均匀态方程和壁面密度分布的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典密度泛函理论（cDFT）的核心挑战在于精确构建自由能泛函，传统解析泛函（如基本测度理论）在描述硬体流体的结构关联时精度有限。作者希望利用神经网络从模拟数据中直接学习单粒子直接关联泛函，探索在全三维密度分布上进行神经密度泛函计算的可行性，并明确向连续流体推广面临的关键挑战。

Method: 以简单立方晶格上边长为3的平行硬立方体为模型系统：(1) 使用巨正则蒙特卡洛模拟在随机外势中生成训练数据；(2) 构建全卷积网络学习单粒子直接关联泛函 c^(1)[ρ]；(3) 训练与推理均使用完整密度剖面，引入输出位点的随机伯努利掩码实现高效全剖面训练；(4) 对第一层卷积核在立方点群的48种旋转和反射下取平均，无需数据增强即可施加精确的立方等变性；(5) 将学习到的神经泛函与独立模拟数据及Lafuente-Cuesta晶格基本测度泛函进行系统对比。

Result: (1) 神经泛函在均匀态方程和平面硬壁密度剖面上相比晶格基本测度泛函有显著提升；(2) 对于固定粒子周围的各向异性对分布，两种泛函都能再现主堆积壳层，但相对精度依赖于晶格方向；(3) 研究明确了向连续流体推广的三大核心挑战：高精度全维度训练数据的获取、热力学一致性的保证以及结构关联的准确描述。

Conclusion: 本研究成功展示了在完整三维密度剖面上进行神经密度泛函计算的可行性，证明了全卷积网络结合物理对称性约束（立方等变性）可以有效学习非均匀流体的自由能泛函。但向连续流体的推广仍需解决训练数据精度、热力学自洽性和长程结构关联等关键问题。

Abstract: We construct a neural classical density functional that acts directly on unrestricted three-dimensional density profiles. As a computationally tractable test bed, we consider parallel hard cubes of side length three on a simple cubic lattice. A fully convolutional network learns the one-body direct-correlation functional $c^{(1)}[ρ]$ from data obtained with grand-canonical Monte Carlo simulations in randomized external potentials. Complete profiles are used during both training and inference; a stochastic Bernoulli mask on the output sites makes full-profile training effective without explicitly extracting and storing overlapping local density windows. Averaging the first-layer kernels over all 48 rotations and reflections of the cubic point group additionally imposes exact cubic equivariance without data augmentation. We compare the learned functional with independent simulation data and with the lattice fundamental-measure functional of Lafuente and Cuesta. The neural functional markedly improves the homogeneous equation of state and the density profile at a planar hard wall. For the anisotropic pair distribution around a fixed particle, both functionals reproduce the principal packing shells, with their relative accuracy depending on crystallographic direction. These results demonstrate neural density-functional calculations on complete three-dimensional profiles while also identifying accurate full-dimensional training data, thermodynamic consistency, and structural correlations as the central challenges for extensions to continuum fluids.

</details>


### [110] [Out-of-equilibrium inhomogeneous XX chains: Exact results and the hydrodynamic limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08205)
*Vincenzo Alba,Federico Rottoli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了XX链在阶梯型磁场下的非平衡动力学，通过Jordan-Wigner变换映射为非均匀紧束缚链。利用Fourier-Laplace变换将问题化为标准Riemann-Hilbert问题，获得了多个初态下费米子两点关联函数的精确解析表达式，并在流体力学极限下给出了仅依赖于有效透射系数的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一维量子多体系统在非均匀外场下的非平衡动力学行为，特别是量子淬火后关联函数的时空演化，旨在获得超越数值模拟的解析理解。

Method: 将XX链通过Jordan-Wigner变换映射为非均匀紧束缚费米子链；采用Fourier变换与Laplace变换相结合的方法，将关联函数的求解转化为单位圆上的标准Riemann-Hilbert边值问题；在流体力学极限（x,y,t→∞且比值固定）下提取渐进行为。

Result: 获得了多种初态（包括均匀和非均匀乘积态）下费米子两点关联函数的精确解析表达式；在流体力学极限下，关联函数由仅依赖越过原点处的有效透射系数的显式公式给出；数值模拟验证了流体力学极限下解析预测的准确性，并观察到了有限时间修正。

Conclusion: 通过将动力学问题转化为Riemann-Hilbert问题，成功获得了XX链在阶梯磁场下量子淬火后关联函数的解析解，流体力学极限下的简洁显式公式与数值结果高度吻合，为理解非均匀量子多体系统的非平衡普适行为提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We study the out-of-equilibrium dynamics in the XX chain with step-like magnetic field, which maps to an inhomogeneous tight-binding chain after Jordan-Wigner transformation. We obtain exact analytic expressions for the fermionic two-point correlation function after a quantum quench from several initial product states, both homogeneous and inhomogeneous ones. This is achieved by using a combination of Fourier and Laplace transforms, which al low us to map the problem to a standard Riemann-Hilbert problem on the unit circle. For arbitrary positions and times the correlators are not expressed in terms of elementary functions. However, in the hydrodynamic limit $x,y,t\to\infty$ with fixed ratios, we provide explicit formulas that depend only on the effective transmission coefficient across the origin. We benchmark our analytic predictions against exact numerical simulations and find excellent agreement in the hydrodynamic limit, apart from finite-time corrections.

</details>


### [111] [High-Capacity Generalized Hopfield Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08226)
*Victor Galitski*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入定义在黎曼流形（特别是SU(d)对称空间）上的广义Hopfield网络，利用李代数方法将其转化为辅助希尔伯特空间中的线性代数问题。结果表明，随着d增大，临界容量较传统向量网络提升近一个数量级；回忆机制对应于尖峰矩阵顶端特征向量的神经元对齐，且可通过Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert动力学自然实现。论文还展示了SU(3)彩色图像编解码、网络的量子化及其与Sachdev-Ye玻璃模型的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Hopfield网络的存储容量有限，且神经元变量通常为二值或实向量。作者旨在将神经元推广到连续黎曼流形上的变量，以期大幅提升临界存储容量，同时利用几何约束和对称性带来的数学结构简化分析与回忆机制。

Method: 将记忆与神经元定义在SU(d)对称空间上；采用Galitski (2011) 的李代数方法，将受非线性几何约束的经典神经网络精确地映射到辅助希尔伯特空间中的线性代数描述；利用数值模拟与副本(replica)解析技术计算临界容量；通过尖峰随机矩阵理论分析回忆机制（顶端特征向量对齐）；引入广义Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert动力学实现物理回忆；最后对网络进行量子化，分析其多体能谱的Wigner-Dyson统计特性。

Result: SU(d) Hopfield网络的临界容量从d=3开始比向量网络提升近一个数量级，并随d快速增长；回忆对应于尖峰矩阵顶端特征向量的神经元对齐，较其他连续变量模型更不易受随机矩阵串扰影响；SU(3)彩色图像编解码协议验证了回忆能力；量子化后模型退化为Sachdev-Ye型玻璃模型，能谱呈现暗带与记忆带，后者具有掩盖Hebbian数据的混沌Wigner-Dyson能级统计。

Conclusion: 将Hopfield网络推广到SU(d)对称空间上可显著提升存储容量，李代数映射提供了优雅的线性代数框架；物理动力学与量子化分别揭示了自然的回忆机制及与量子玻璃模型的深层联系，为新型神经形态计算硬件实现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Generalized Hopfield networks are introduced where memories and neurons are continuous variables that lie on a Riemannian manifold. We explicitly focus on symmetric spaces associated with the special unitary groups SU(d), and use both numerical and analytical (replica) techniques to demonstrate an almost order of magnitude enhancement in critical capacity over the vector networks starting with d=3 and further rapidly growing with d. To circumvent the non-linear geometric constraints, we use a Lie algebraic method [following V. Galitski, Phys. Rev. A 84, 012118 (2011)] to exactly describe the classical neural network in terms of linear algebra in an auxiliary Hilbert space. It is shown that in contrast to the traditional Hopfield networks, memory recall in SU(d) Hopfields corresponds to neuron alignment along a top eigenvector of a spiked matrix, which is less susceptible to random matrix crosstalk than other models with continuous neuron variables. Physical platforms to realize SU(d) Hopfields are briefly discussed and physical (in addition to algorithmic) recall mechanism is demonstrated, where memory recovery occurs naturally through generalized Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert dynamics. To illustrate SU(3) memory recall, we introduce a color (RGB) image encoding/decoding protocol and explicitly run image recovery on corrupted cues. Finally, we quantize the generalized Hopfields which are shown to reduce to Sachdev-Ye glassy type of models. Their many-body spectra generally feature two types of dark and memory bands, where the latter exhibits chaotic Wigner-Dyson level statistics that hides Hebbian data.

</details>


### [112] [Persistence probability based dynamics and phase diagrams in biased q-voter models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08324)
*Amit Pradhan,Pratik Mullick,Parongama Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种非线性q-voter模型中意见的持久性概率，即个体不改变初始意见的倾向。通过平均场理论和数值模拟，发现持久性概率的长期行为要么是饱和，要么是指数衰减，并据此给出了参数空间的相图，揭示了持久性与模型不动点行为之间的非平凡关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性概率是意见动力学中衡量个体不改变初始意见倾向的重要指标，但在非线性q-voter模型中尚未被系统研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索两种非线性q-voter模型中持久性概率的长期行为及其与模型稳态之间的关联。

Method: 采用平均场理论进行解析推导，结合数值模拟，分别计算两种意见类型的持久性概率。研究覆盖了不同参数范围，对应已知的稳态状态，并通过分析持久性概率的长期行为（饱和或指数衰减）构建参数空间的相图。

Result: 持久性概率的长期行为表现为两种形式：饱和或可用指数形式近似的衰减，取决于所选参数。基于此构建了两个模型的相图，相图中的区域与对应模型不动点的行为存在强相关性，这一关联在持久性概率的语境下是非平凡的。

Conclusion: 持久性概率的渐近行为与模型不动点的稳定性特征密切相关，相图为理解非线性q-voter模型中意见持久性提供了系统的分类框架。这一发现揭示了持久性概率与动力学系统不动点之间非平凡的内在联系。

Abstract: Persistence probability in opinion dynamics models estimates the tendency of the agents not to change their initial opinion till the present time. Here we consider two nonlinear q-voter models with binary opinions, where the dynamics are governed by a biased choice when the q panel is not unanimous. The models are studied for different parameter ranges corresponding to the known stationary states. Mean field theory and numerical simulations are used to compute the persistence probability for the two types of opinion separately. The long time behavior in general is either a saturation or a decay that can be approximated by an exponential form, depending on the chosen parameters. Based on this, phase diagrams in the parameter space are presented for both the models. The regions in the phase diagrams indicate a strong correlation with the behavior of fixed points in the corresponding models, which is non-trivial as far as the persistence probability is concerned.

</details>


### [113] [Nonisospectral Integrability and Exact Current Fluctuations in the Two-Dimensional SSEP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08480)
*Tingfei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 二维对称简单排他过程中，通过圆形计数边界的退火电流涨落的大偏差统计。利用宏观涨落理论的凸性和旋转平均，将二维变分问题约化为径向问题，再通过变量变换和散射方法得到标度累积量生成函数的闭合表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维SSEP中通过圆形边界的电流大偏差行为，此前结果多限于一维或矩形几何，圆形边界下的二维退火电流统计尚缺乏严格解析结果。

Method: 基于宏观涨落理论(MFT)建立变分问题；利用MFT作用的凸性和旋转平均证明极小化子可选取为径向对称；通过变量变换将径向问题转化为半直线上的非等谱问题；结合散射构造与标量分解，导出闭合解。

Result: 得到了标度累积量生成函数(SCGF)的闭合表达式，从而完整给出了圆形计数边界下退火电流大偏差统计的解析结果。

Conclusion: 通过MFT、对称性约化及散射方法，成功解决了二维SSEP中圆形被动计数边界下的退火电流涨落问题，将一维结果推广到具有旋转对称性的二维几何。

Abstract: We study annealed current fluctuations in the two-dimensional symmetric simple exclusion process across a circular passive counting boundary. The initial density is $ρ_1$ inside a disk of radius $R$ and $ρ_2$ outside, and the observable is the net decrease of the particle number in the disk over a finite time. Using convexity of the macroscopic fluctuation theory action and rotational averaging, we show that the minimizer of the full two-dimensional variational problem may be chosen radially symmetric. For the resulting radial problem, a suitable change of variables leads to a nonisospectral formulation on the half-line. Combining the associated scattering construction with a scalar factorization, we obtain a closed expression for the scaled cumulant generating function and hence the annealed large-deviation statistics of the current.

</details>


### [114] [A Shared Observation Shields Collective Fluctuations while Preserving Local Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08358)
*Hu Cang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过Girsanov路径变换，精确计算了在共享观测记录条件下，产生该记录的粒子群体的统计性质。证明条件化在独立联合分布上仅乘一个沿集体方向的中心平方惩罚项，导致成对协方差为O(z⁻¹)、互信息为O(z⁻²)，但大量弱关联相干叠加产生有限的集体涨落抑制（Schur屏蔽）。由此为动态异质性提供了一个可计算的基线：χ₄中超出该基线的部分才是真正的协同信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体接近玻璃化转变时，动力学异质性通过四点极化率χ₄表征，但解释χ₄的增长存在一个根本性困难：实验记录（如标记粒子轨迹、重叠函数或平均场）由其所描述的同一批粒子产生，这意味着观测本身对产生记录的群体施加了条件化效应。需要精确计算这种"共享记录条件化"对统计量的贡献，以区分真正的协同效应与条件化本身带来的伪影。

Method: 作者考虑一个标记粒子及其z个邻居组成的笼（cage）作为引导例子，利用Girsanov路径变换对一类随机观测系统进行精确的条件化分析。核心结果是：条件化在z条轨迹的独立联合分布上仅乘一个沿记录可见的单一集体方向的中心平方惩罚项。由此导出Schur屏蔽公式 D − C = −C²(aI + C)⁻¹ ≼ 0，并利用一个精确可解的Brownian模型进行校准。

Result: （1）任意固定粒子对近似保持独立，协方差为O(z⁻¹)，互信息为O(z⁻²)，满足"混沌传播"性质；（2）z(z−1)个弱成对关联相干叠加，产生有限的集体涨落抑制，即Schur屏蔽；（3）条件化本身对条件化系综的极化率贡献一个可计算的、非正的量，因此真正的协同信号是实测χ₄超出该基线而非超出零的部分。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态异质性提供了一个可计算的基线：χ₄中条件化本身贡献的部分可以通过理论精确计算并扣除，剩余的超出量才是真实的协同合作信号。这一比较可直接用现有模拟数据完成，为玻璃化转变中动态异质性的定量解释提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: As a liquid approaches its glass transition, its dynamics turns heterogeneous: mobile and immobile regions coexist, and the four-point susceptibility $χ_4$ that quantifies this heterogeneity grows sharply. Interpreting that growth is subtle, because the collective signals experiments record, such as a tagged particle's trajectory, an overlap function, or a mean field, are generated by the same particles they describe. Here we compute exactly what conditioning on such a shared record does to the population that produced it, for a broad class of stochastically observed systems; the guiding example is a tagged particle and the cage of $z$ neighbors that drives its force history. Using a Girsanov path transformation, we prove that the conditioning multiplies the independent joint law of the $z$ trajectories by exactly one term: a centered-square penalty along the single collective direction the record can see. Any fixed pair of particles stays nearly independent, with covariance falling as $O(z^{-1})$ and mutual information as $O(z^{-2})$, the property known as propagation of chaos, yet the $z(z-1)$ weak pair correlations add coherently into a finite suppression of collective fluctuations, the Schur shield $D - C = -C^2(aI + C)^{-1} \preceq 0$. An exactly solvable Brownian model calibrates the construction. The physical consequence is a calculable baseline for dynamical heterogeneity: conditioning itself contributes a computable, nonpositive amount to the susceptibility of a conditioned ensemble, so the genuine cooperative signal is the excess of the measured $χ_4$ over this baseline rather than over zero, a comparison that existing simulation data can already perform.

</details>


### [115] [Solving the Gibbs Paradox by Local Free Space and Collision Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08457)
*Chuang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需借助量子力学全同粒子概念来解决吉布斯佯谬的方法。作者基于局域自由空间假设和碰撞势能，利用正则系综重新推导了气体混合熵增公式，其中包含分子质量、有效半径和碰撞特征时间等参数，使熵增依赖于气体种类，从而真正解决了吉布斯佯谬。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统吉布斯佯谬的解决依赖于量子力学中全同粒子的概念和N!因子，但作者认为气体分子在不同区域是可以区分的，无需引入N!因子。作者旨在从经典统计物理的角度，基于分子碰撞的物理机制，给出一个使混合熵增依赖于气体性质的真正解决方案，而非仅仅是概念上的重新解释。

Method: 作者提出两个核心假设：（1）局域自由空间假设——每个分子在每一瞬时具有局域的自由运动体积；（2）碰撞势能不可忽略——分子间碰撞是达到平衡的主要驱动力。基于这两点，采用正则系综方法重新推导气体混合熵增公式，引入分子质量、有效半径和碰撞特征时间等气体种类相关的参数。

Result: 推导出的混合熵增公式包含了分子质量、有效半径和碰撞特征时间等随气体种类变化的参数，使得熵增不再与气体性质无关，从而真正解决了吉布斯佯谬。

Conclusion: 吉布斯佯谬可以在经典统计物理框架内，通过考虑局域自由空间和碰撞势能来真正解决，无需依赖量子力学的全同粒子概念。该方法使混合熵增对气体性质敏感，是对佯谬的根本性解决而非概念性重述。

Abstract: This paper argues that the Gibbs paradox can be resolved without using the concept of identical particles in quantum mechanics. The molecules in different regions of the gas can be distinguished, so there is no need to introduce the N! factor. For each molecule, the volume of its free movement space is local at every instant. Moreover, collisions are the primary way of interaction between gas molecules and the fundamental driving force for reaching equilibrium. The potential energy during collisions cannot be ignored. Based on the local free space assumption and collision potential energy, this paper uses the canonical ensemble method to rederive the entropy increase formula for gas mixtures. It includes parameters such as molecular mass, effective radius, and collision characteristic time, which vary with the type of gas molecules. This solves the problem that the entropy increase of gas mixtures is independent of gas properties, that is, it truly resolves the Gibbs paradox instead of providing a new conceptual explanation.

</details>


### [116] [Scalene Yang--Baxter triples as a source of hidden symmetries beyond the ordinary Yang--Baxter equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09081)
*Pramod Padmanabhan,Somnath Maity,Vladimir Korepin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由非编织不等边Yang-Baxter三元组构造的最近邻非厄米自旋链。其局域哈密顿量密度违反差分形式的Reshetikhin条件，导致标准转移矩阵互不对易，但不等边Yang-Baxter关系保证其与三元组中第三个成员构造的转移矩阵之间存在交叉对易性。后者在偶数周期链上生成一个非平庸的交错幂零对称性及其守恒层级，为超越传统自对易转移矩阵框架的代数对称性发现机制提供了范例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Yang-Baxter方程的可积性框架要求转移矩阵族在谱参数不同时彼此对易，这依赖于R矩阵满足Reshetikhin条件。作者旨在探索：当该条件被违反时，不等边Yang-Baxter三元组是否仍能充当发现隐藏对称性的代数机制，从而拓展可积性理论的传统边界。

Method: 从精确的非编织不等边Yang-Baxter三元组出发，选取其中一个成员定义最近邻非厄米自旋链的局域哈密顿量密度。验证其违反差分形式及一般非差分形式的Reshetikhin条件，证明无法由可微齐次正则解实现。利用不等边Yang-Baxter关系推导与第三成员转移矩阵的交叉对易性，对任意链长解析求值，并在偶数周期链上揭示其作为交错幂零对称性生成函数的结构。

Result: （1）局域哈密顿量密度违反Reshetikhin条件，常规转移矩阵互不对易；（2）与三元组第三成员构造的转移矩阵存在交叉对易性；（3）该转移矩阵在偶数周期链上是交错幂零对称性的有限生成函数，整个守恒层级由单一对称性生成的代数构成，并非代数独立的扩展族。

Conclusion: 不等边Yang-Baxter三元组可充当超越传统自对易转移矩阵框架的代数对称性发现机制。该机制不产生大量代数独立守恒量，但仍能揭示非平庸的隐藏对称性结构，为量子可积系统与非厄米系统交叉领域提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study a nearest-neighbor non-Hermitian spin chain obtained from one member of an exact non-braided scalene Yang--Baxter triple. Its local Hamiltonian density violates both the difference-form Reshetikhin condition and its general non-difference counterpart, obstructing its realization by a differentiable homogeneous regular solution of the ordinary Yang--Baxter equation. The transfer matrices constructed from the regular member do not commute among themselves at distinct spectral parameters. Nevertheless, the scalene Yang--Baxter relation implies cross-commutativity with another transfer matrix constructed from the third member of the scalene triple. We evaluate the latter for arbitrary chain length and show that, on even periodic chains, it is a finite generating function of a non-obvious staggered nilpotent symmetry. The resulting conserved hierarchy belongs entirely to the algebra generated by this single symmetry and hence does not constitute an extensive family of algebraically independent charges. Nevertheless, this example demonstrates that scalene Yang--Baxter triples can act as an algebraic symmetry-discovery mechanism beyond the ordinary self-commuting transfer-matrix framework.

</details>


### [117] [Lee-Yang Theory Guided Force Field Refinement Based on Phase Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08546)
*Bin Jin,Qijun Ye,Xinzheng Li,Kuang Yu,Shenzhen Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种基于李-杨相变理论的通用力场自动优化框架，以配分函数模量作为相图引导的优化目标，无需序参量或响应性质，即可统一处理各类非连续相变，并在Lennard-Jones势和Cu嵌入原子势上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前力场开发中，以相图为目标的优化通常依赖系统特定的序参量或响应性质来表征相变点，缺乏通用性，且数值上显式求解李-杨零点困难。需要一种普适、数值稳定的相图引导力场优化方法。

Method: 利用李-杨相变理论，直接以配分函数模量（在靠近实轴处求值）作为优化目标函数。该方法基于李-杨零点与相变的关联，在实轴附近评估模量既能捕捉相变信息，又避免显式求解零点的数值困难。整个过程无需系统特定的序参量或响应性质，通过自动微分实现力场参数的迭代优化。

Result: 在Lennard-Jones势（涵盖气-液和固-液转变）和基于实验熔化曲线的Cu嵌入原子势上验证。优化后的力场在所有体系中均显著改善了目标相图的复现能力。对Cu而言，优化不仅改善了相图，还同时提升了焓和热容等超出优化目标的观测量的预测精度。

Conclusion: 李-杨相变理论可作为当代力场开发的实用工具，该框架具有普适性和数值稳定性，有望广泛应用于各类材料体系的力场优化。

Abstract: We propose a general framework for automatic force field refinement guided by phase diagrams, grounded in Lee-Yang phase transition theory. The central idea is to directly use the partition function modulus as a phase-diagram-guided optimization target. Evaluating the modulus at points close to the real axis, where the Lee-Yang zeros are mostly associated with the phase transition, is more physically meaningful and avoids the numerical difficulty of explicitly solving for the zeros. This approach requires no system-specific order parameters or response properties for characterizing phase transition points, making it universal across various discontinuous phase transitions and material systems. We validate the method on refining parameters of a Lennard-Jones potential covering both gas-liquid and solid-liquid transitions, and a Cu embedded-atom method potential based on experimental melting curves. The refined force fields reproduce the target phase diagrams with significant improvement across all systems. For Cu, the refinement simultaneously improves predictions of enthalpy and heat capacity, which are observables beyond the optimization target. These results establish Lee-Yang theory as a practical tool for contemporary force field development.

</details>


### [118] [Reliability-Safety Trade-off in AI Distillation: A Renormalization-Group Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08572)
*Y. M. Du,Miao-Miao Yi,Tan-Ji Zhou,C. P. Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文采用统计力学中的粗粒化模型，将知识蒸馏中的学生行为（回答/拒答）描述为两个宏观态，并提出由单一参数K（危险辨别能力）控制的可靠性-安全性权衡关系。进一步将多代蒸馏类比为重整化群流，揭示K跨代演化的三临界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示知识蒸馏不仅传递任务能力，更传递响应倾向、拒答策略、错误边界与潜在安全偏好的行为遗传现象，并为这一现象建立形式化的理论框架。

Method: 基于统计力学构建粗粒化模型，将学生回答与拒答决策设为两个宏观态；教师诱导有效场重塑学生自由能景观；导出由单一参数K（危险辨别能力）控制的可靠性-安全性权衡关系；将多代蒸馏建模为重整化群式迭代变换，分析K的跨代流动与临界行为。

Result: 推导出的可靠性-安全性权衡关系与拒答令牌数据（arXiv:2412.06748）一致；教师危险辨别能力强则学生可达的可靠性与安全性上界更高，反之受限；多代蒸馏下K的流动呈现三临界结构，区分为K衰减、稳定传输和阈值依赖遗传三种相态，并给出可检验的标度预测。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏中的行为遗传可通过统计力学粗粒化模型有效描述，危险辨别能力K是刻画蒸馏质量与安全对齐的核心序参量；多代蒸馏类比重整化群流，为理解和控制模型族群演化提供了可检验的理论预言。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers more than task competence: it also transmits response propensities, refusal policies, error boundaries, and latent safety biases. We formulate this behavioral inheritance as a coarse-graining model grounded in statistical mechanics, in which the student's answer and refusal decisions define two macrostates, while the teacher induces an effective field that reshapes the student's free-energy landscape. The model yields a reliability-safety trade-off relation controlled by a single parameter K, which we term the hazard discrimination capability. The predicted trade-off is consistent with refusal-token data [arXiv: 2412.06748]. In knowledge distillation, a teacher with strong hazard discrimination improves the student's attainable reliability and safety, whereas poor discrimination limits the attainable trade-off. Repeated distillation acts as an iterated renormalization-group-like transformation, under which K follows a flow across generations. The flow exhibits a tricritical structure separating regimes of K loss, stable transmission, and threshold-dependent inheritance, and yields testable scaling predictions for multigenerational distillation.

</details>


### [119] [Retained hidden excess generates memory in price-limited markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08625)
*Debraj Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个最小化随机隐状态模型，用于描述股票在涨跌停板制度下未观测到的超额收益如何在次日部分保留，从而产生记忆效应。模型预测：涨停或跌停后次日同向收益均值与涨跌幅宽度成正比，反向触及对面板的概率则被幂律抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股票日内收益常受交易所设定的涨跌停板限制，超出部分被截断而无法观测。现有研究较少关注这种截断产生的"隐藏超额"如何跨日传递并影响后续价格行为。作者旨在填补这一空白，从随机过程角度建模涨跌停板制度对收益率序列记忆性及尾部行为的影响。

Method: 构建一个最小化随机隐状态模型：每日驱动冲击为独立同分布的对称正则变化尾部分布；超出涨跌停板的部分按固定比例保留至次日作为"隐状态"叠加；在宽涨跌幅极限下，利用单主导冲击近似推导涨停/跌停事件后次日收益的条件均值、触及同向与反向板的概率；通过模拟验证解析预测，并用真实受限股票数据做定性对比。

Result: （1）平稳隐状态收益保留了噪声的尾部指数，但尾部幅度增大；（2）宽涨跌幅极限下，涨停/跌停事件可由单一主导冲击刻画；（3）触及板后次日同向收益均值与涨跌幅宽度成正比；（4）次日再次触及同向板的概率趋于有限值；（5）次日触及反向板需要反向极端冲击，概率被幂律抑制；（6）模拟支持解析结果，实证数据在定性上一致。

Conclusion: 涨跌停板制度通过截断超额收益的跨日保留机制，在收益率序列中引入了可量化的记忆效应。该模型为理解价格限制制度下的市场微观结构和极端事件后的短期动量效应提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The daily return of a stock is often restricted to an exchange-imposed band to curb extreme fluctuations. Any attempted price movement beyond this band is clipped, leaving an unobserved excess. We introduce a minimal stochastic latent-state model in which a fraction of this hidden excess is retained for the next day. This retention generates memory, even though the daily stochastic driving shocks are independent. For symmetric driving shocks with regularly varying tails, the stationary latent return preserves the tail index of the noise, but has an enhanced tail amplitude. In the wide-band limit, a close of the daily return at either limit of the band admits a single-dominant-shock description. We show that after such an event, the mean return on the following day has the same sign and grows proportionally to the band width, while the probability of reaching the same limit again approaches a finite value. Reaching the opposite band limit on the following day requires a second extreme shock of opposite sign and is power-law suppressed. Simulations support these analytical predictions. Empirical data from stocks subject to daily price limits are qualitatively consistent with the predicted same-sign response and its increase across wider price bands.

</details>


### [120] [A Systematic Comparison of q-Gaussian Fitting Methods across Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08666)
*Facundo Máximo Abaca,Darío Javier Zamora,Bruno S. Zossi,Ana G. Elias*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种估计非广延统计中q参数的方法：PDF直接非线性拟合、q对数线性化拟合和CDF数值拟合。研究发现q估计对直方图分箱高度敏感，而基于CDF的方法因无需分箱而提供更稳定的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非广延统计在处理具有长程相关和重尾分布的非平衡态非线性现象方面优于传统玻尔兹曼-吉布斯统计，但非广延参数q的估计方法因学科而异且缺乏统一标准，亟需系统性的跨学科比较来确立可靠方法。

Method: 作者对三类拟合法进行跨学科系统比较：(1) 概率密度函数(PDF)的直接非线性拟合；(2) 利用q对数函数进行线性化拟合；(3) 累积分布函数(CDF)的数值拟合。分析涵盖地球物理学、空间天气和经济学等多个领域的实际数据，并重点考察离散化（直方图分箱）对估计结果的关键影响。

Result: q参数估计对直方图分箱策略高度敏感，不同分箱方式可导致显著不同的估计结果。基于CDF的拟合方法由于不依赖分箱，在所分析的数据集上提供了更为稳健的q估计值。

Conclusion: 基于CDF的无分箱方法为重尾分布参数的正确确定提供了更稳定的替代方案，对多个学科领域中非广延统计的推广应用具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Nature exhibits a wide variety of nonlinear phenomena characterized by strong fluctuations that push the system away from equilibrium. Recent studies have shown that non-extensive statistics are more suitable for analyzing these phenomena, as they can capture long-range correlations and heavy-tailed distributions that conventional Boltzmann-Gibbs statistics cannot represent. However, estimating the non-extensiveness parameter ($q$) is not trivial, and methods vary significantly across disciplines.
  This paper presents a systematic and interdisciplinary comparison of three fitting methods: direct nonlinear fitting of the probability distribution function (PDF), linearization using the q-logarithm function, and numerical fitting of the cumulative distribution function (CDF). We applied this analysis to various phenomena, from areas such as geophysics, space weather and economics, analyzing the critical impact of discretization. The results show that $q$ estimates are highly sensitive to histogram binning, while the CDF-based method provides a bin-free alternative that yields more stable estimates for the datasets analyzed here. This contribution provides relevant information for the search for a method that allows the correct determination of the parameters of heavy-tailed distributions in various disciplines.

</details>


### [121] [Pseudo-Distributions: Thermodynamic Geometry and an Empirical Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09297)
*Negin Bayrami,Hosein Mohammadzadeh,Hamzeh Agahi,Hossein Mehri-Dehnavi,Zahra Ebadi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于g-演算发展了一套自洽的伪分析框架，系统推导出伪对数和伪指数结构，并引入一类新的伪分布族，统一了经典和非延广统计力学。通过Ruppeiner度规研究热力学几何，发现曲率标量在微扰首阶仅由生成元形变参数主导。应用于原油价格波动分析，该模型在描述重尾特征方面优于传统基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非延广统计力学中的形变统计分布缺乏统一的伪分析构造框架，需要一种基于g-演算的系统性方法来生成形变统计分布，并研究其热力学几何性质。

Method: 以单调生成函数映射标准代数运算，系统构造伪对数和伪指数结构；引入新伪分布族推广非延广统计力学；在平衡流形上使用Ruppeiner度规进行热力学几何分析；以经典极限为基点做微扰展开；利用信息准则对WTI原油价格日绝对偏差数据进行模型比较。

Result: 新伪分布族在概率密度和累积分布层面统一了经典统计与非延广统计作为极限情形；微扰分析表明热力学标量曲率首阶仅与生成元形变参数相关，非延广参数退耦合；在原油价格波动实证中，该模型对高频金融波动的重尾特征拟合优于标准基准。

Conclusion: g-演算为生成形变统计及其几何分析提供了一种灵活且具有物理基础的数学工具，所提出的伪分布在高频金融数据建模中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: We develop a consistent pseudo-analytic framework based on $g$-calculus for constructing deformed statistical distributions. By mapping standard algebraic operations through a monotone generator function, we systematically derive the associated pseudo-logarithmic and pseudo-exponential structures. Applying this formalism, we introduce a new family of pseudo-distributions that generalizes nonextensive statistical mechanics at both the probability density and cumulative distribution levels, recovering classical and standard nonextensive statistics as limiting cases. We investigate the thermodynamic geometry of the proposed models using the Ruppeiner metric on the equilibrium manifold. A perturbative analysis around the classical limit reveals that, to leading order, the thermodynamic scalar curvature is governed solely by the generator deformation parameter, while the nonextensivity parameter remains decoupled. To evaluate the empirical robustness of the framework, we apply the model to analyze the absolute deviations of daily West Texas Intermediate crude oil prices from their hundred-day moving average. Model comparison based on information criteria demonstrates that the proposed pseudo-distributions provide a superior description of these high-frequency financial fluctuations and their heavy-tailed characteristics compared to standard benchmarks. These results suggest that $g$-calculus offers a flexible and physically grounded mathematical tool for generating deformed statistics and analyzing their geometric properties.

</details>


### [122] [Ensemble-level loopy message passing with generalized-edge closure for percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09397)
*L. -H. Wang,Y. -M. Du*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义边统计的循环消息传递框架，用于预测高度聚类网络的渗流阈值。通过引入广义边闭合系数(GECC)作为先验诊断指标，该框架能够在不依赖渗流计算的情况下评估近似可靠性，在合成和真实网络中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理高度聚类网络时存在局限：传统树状消息传递依赖无环独立性假设，而短环路破坏了这一假设。已有补救方案要么依赖随机图系综中的预设局部模体，要么仅针对单一网络的拓扑结构，缺乏对任意连接模式的系综层面处理。因此需要一种能系统处理任意聚类模式的系综级消息传递方法。

Method: 作者开发了一种基于广义边统计的循环消息传递框架，用于随机聚类图系综。该方法利用广义边刻画不同节点邻域之间的重叠模式，通过逐级扩大节点邻域尺寸构建递进精化的近似方案。一阶近似恢复已有方程，二阶近似给出新的精化阈值预测。此外，引入广义边闭合系数(GECC)来量化广义边的内部一致性，作为近似可靠性的先验诊断指标。

Result: 在合成网络和真实网络上的实验表明，二阶近似的渗流阈值预测优于低阶近似；GECC能够有效捕捉影响渗流阈值的关键结构特征；与蒙特卡洛模拟对比验证了框架的有效性。GECC无需任何渗流计算即可作为近似可靠性的先验诊断。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了基于系综的循环消息传递方法，为大规模聚类网络中的渗流阈值预测提供了一条高效途径。GECC作为结构一致性指标，为方法适用性提供了可解释的先验判据。

Abstract: Predicting the percolation threshold of highly clustered networks from local statistics remains difficult, because short loops break the independence assumption underlying tree-like message passing. Existing remedies address loopy connectivity either through prescribed local motifs in random-graph ensembles or through a single network's realized topology, leaving an ensemble-level treatment of arbitrary connectivity patterns absent. Here, we develop a loopy message-passing framework for random clustered graph ensembles based on generalized-edge statistics, which characterize overlap patterns among the neighborhoods of different nodes. This yields a progressively refined approximation scheme based on neighborhoods of increasing size around each node. The low-order approximations recover previous equations for random network ensembles, and the new result that yields refined threshold prediction is developed by the second-order approximation. We show that the effectiveness of this framework depends not only on short-cycle density but also on the internal consistency of generalized edges. To diagnose this effectiveness, we introduce the generalized-edge closure coefficient (GECC) to quantify this consistency. Because GECC is computed entirely from local statistics and does not rely on any percolation calculation, it serves as an a priori diagnostic for the reliability of the approximation. Using synthetic and real networks, the threshold is evaluated via the second-order and lower-order approximations. Comparisons with Monte Carlo simulations show that GECC captures key structural features that strongly affect the percolation threshold. These results establish ensemble-based loopy message passing as an efficient route for predicting the percolation threshold in large clustered networks.

</details>


### [123] [Sampling Free Energy Landscapes of Ionic Colloidal Crystal Systems using Machine-Learned Proxy Collective Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09714)
*Michael S. Chen,Stefano Sacanna,Glen M. Hocky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文利用SE(3)-等变Transformer网络作为自由能计算的代理模型，克服了传统有序参数无法区分胶体晶体结构的局限性，实现了对不同热力学亚稳态晶体结构的相对自由能计算，并展示了如何通过调节表面电势来调控胶体自组装的目标晶体结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物刷包覆的带电胶体可自组装成多种晶体结构，但实验和模拟中常观察到多种热力学（亚）稳态结构的分布。传统晶体有序参数无法有效区分不同晶体结构及无定形聚集体，而局部环境相似性描述符虽能区分这些状态，却因计算开销过大无法用于有偏分子动力学模拟。因此需要一种高效且具备区分能力的替代方案来量化自由能景观。

Method: 研究者采用机器学习相互作用势领域的方法，使用SE(3)-等变Transformer网络作为高效可评估的机器学习代理模型。该模型能够替代昂贵的局部环境相似性描述符，嵌入到自由能计算流程中，从而计算不同胶体晶体结构之间的相对自由能。此外，通过调节正负胶体的表面电势（同时保持相同的吸引能量），探索了实验可调参数对晶体结构偏好的影响。

Result: SE(3)-等变Transformer网络成功区分了相关亚稳态胶体晶体结构，且计算效率远高于传统局部环境相似性描述符。通过该方法，作者能够计算可及胶体结构的相对自由能随实验可调物理参数的变化，并展示了在保持吸引能量不变的条件下，改变正负胶体表面电势可以改变哪种晶体结构在热力学上更受青睐。

Conclusion: SE(3)-等变Transformer网络为胶体自组装系统的自由能景观计算提供了一种高效且准确的工具，使得研究者能够理性地选择实验条件来优先定向目标晶体结构。该方法为通过调节表面电势等"物理旋钮"来精准调控胶体晶体的自组装路径提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Charged colloids coated with a polymer brush can be designed to preferentially self-assemble into different crystal structures by varying easy-to-tune experimental conditions. For a given set of conditions, we have observed in experiments and simulations a distribution of thermodynamically (meta)stable self-assembled crystal structures. Properly quantifying the free energy landscape of these colloidal systems is essential for rationally choosing conditions to preferentially target particular crystal structures. For some of the structures we have formed, standard crystalline order parameters are not able to differentiate between crystals or between crystals and amorphous aggregates. We show that local environment similarity descriptors are able to distinguish the relevant metastable states, but are too expensive for use in biased MD simulations. Here, we adopt an approach from machine-learned interaction potentials showing that SE(3)-equivariant transformer networks can serve as an efficient-to-evaluate machine-learned proxy. As a result, we can compute the relative free energies of accessible colloidal structures as a function of different experimentally-relevant physical knobs that can steer our system between two observed crystal types. As an example application, we then show how changing surface potentials of positive and negative colloids while maintaining the same attractive energy can shift which crystal structure is favored.

</details>

### [124] [Effects of high-pressure synthesis on phase formation and superconducting properties of PrFeAsO1-xFx](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08083)
*Priya Singh,Konrad Kwatek,Tatiana Zajarniuk,Tomasz Cetner,Jan Mizeracki,Shiv J. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了高压高温合成（HP-HTS）对PrFeAsO₁₋ₓFₓ超导体的影响，发现HP-HTS可增强氟掺入、改善相形成和微观结构连通性，在欠掺杂和最佳掺杂组分中分别提升Tc约1 K和6 K，但过掺杂组分中反而抑制超导电性，表明HP-HTS的效果强烈依赖于组分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期高压合成在其他铁基超导体体系中提升超导性能的报道启发，系统研究HP-HTS对Pr1111体系的结构、微观结构、电输运和磁性的影响，以评估该方法在该体系中的普适性和组分依赖性。

Method: 采用0.5 GPa高压、高温合成条件（此前已在其他铁基超导体体系中优化），制备PrFeAsO₁₋ₓFₓ的欠掺杂（x=0.2）、最佳掺杂（x=0.3）和过掺杂（x=0.5）三种组分，通过结构表征、微观结构观察、电输运和磁性测量系统评估HP-HTS的效果。

Result: HP-HTS增强了欠掺杂和最佳掺杂组分中的氟掺入，改善了相形成，产生更致密的微观结构和更好的晶粒连通性；x=0.2的Tc提升约1 K，x=0.3的Tc提升约6 K，但临界电流密度仅有边际改善；欠掺杂组分的电阻转变展宽表明残余结构不均匀性；过掺杂组分则出现杂质相偏析增加和超导电性抑制。

Conclusion: HP-HTS在Pr1111体系中的效果具有强烈的组分依赖性，受氟掺入、相稳定性和微观结构演化之间的相互作用所支配，需进一步优化HP-HTS工艺条件以充分发挥其潜力。

Abstract: Motivated by recent reports of enhanced superconducting performance in several families of iron-based superconductors (IBS) processed by high-pressure (HP) synthesis, we investigate the influence of high gas pressure and high-temperature synthesis (HP-HTS) process on the structural, microstructural, electrical transport, and magnetic properties of Pr-based oxypnictide PrFeAsO1-xFx (Pr1111) using the processing conditions of 0.5 GPa for 1 h previously optimized for other IBS families. Representative underdoped (x = 0.2), optimal doped (x = 0.3), and overdoped (x = 0.5) compositions from the ambient-pressure electronic phase diagram of Pr1111 are selected to evaluate its composition-dependent effects of HP-HTS. The results demonstrate that HP-HTS enhances fluorine incorporation, improves phase formation, and produces a denser microstructure with improved grain connectivity in the underdoped and optimal doped compositions. Magnetic measurements reveal increased in the superconducting transition temperature (Tc) of ~1 K for x = 0.2 and ~6 K for x = 0.3, whereas only a marginal improvement in the critical current density is observed. Electrical resistivity measurements of the underdoped composition show a slight increase in Tc accompanied by a broader resistive transition, indicating residual structural inhomogeneity. In contrast, the overdoped composition exhibits increased impurity phase segregation, accompanied by suppression of superconductivity. These results demonstrate that the effectiveness of HP-HTS in Pr1111 is strongly composition dependent and governed by the interplay among fluorine incorporation, phase stability, and microstructural evolution, highlighting the need for further optimization of the HP-HTS processing conditions.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum Metric Induced Critical Current Anomaly in Flat Band Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08120)
*Zhong C. F. Li,Yuxuan Deng,Dmitri K. Efetov,K. T. Law*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 扭曲双层石墨烯约瑟夫森结中观测到临界电流增加而正常态电导下降的"临界电流反常"现象。作者指出，由于平带抑制传统约瑟夫森电流，量子度规驱动的约瑟夫森电流(QMJC)可解释此反常：当量子度规长度与结长度可比或更长时，存在参数区间使临界电流随电导下降而上升。通过修正的Lieb晶格模型和TBG六带模型验证了理论与实验的高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约瑟夫森结理论中，超导临界电流Ic随正常态电导G增加而增加。然而近期TBG约瑟夫森结实验发现相反趋势——Ic增加时G反而下降。这一"临界电流反常"现象无法被传统理论解释，需要引入新的物理机制。

Method: 作者提出量子度规驱动的约瑟夫森电流(QMJC)作为新机制：平带抑制了传统约瑟夫森电流，此时量子度规长度与结长度可比时，QMJC占主导。将G和Ic均分解为传统贡献和量子度规贡献，论证存在参数区间使G下降时Ic上升。先用修正的Lieb晶格模型进行解析和数值验证，再用TBG六带模型与实验对比。

Result: 修正Lieb晶格模型解析和数值结果均显示出临界电流反常行为。TBG六带模型的计算结果与实验观测高度一致，证实了QMJC机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 临界电流反常为量子度规驱动的约瑟夫森电流(QMJC)提供了强有力的实验证据，揭示了传统约瑟夫森结理论忽略的量子度规效应在平带超导体系中的重要作用。

Abstract: In well-established theories of Josephson junctions, the superconducting critical current \( I_\mathrm{c} \) increases as the normal state conductance \( \mathcal{G} \) increases. However, in a recent experiment in twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) based Josephson junctions, unexpectedly, it was observed that the increase of the critical current is accompanied by a decrease of the normal state conductance. We call this phenomenon the critical current anomaly. In this work, we point out that in the TBG-based Josephson junction, due to the suppression of the conventional Josephson current by the flatness of the band and the quantum metric enabled Josephson current (QMJC), the critical current anomaly can occur. The QMJC appears if the quantum metric length is comparable or longer than the junction length. We show that both \( \mathcal{G} \) and \( I_\mathrm{c} \) have the conventional and the quantum metric contributions, and there are parameter regimes in which \( I_\mathrm{c} \) increases even when \( \mathcal{G} \) decreases. We first demonstrate the critical current anomaly by a simple modified Lieb-lattice model both analytically and numerically. The incredible consistency with the experimental results is demonstrated using a realistic six-band model of twisted bilayer graphene. Therefore, we suggest that the critical current anomaly observed in the experiment provide strong evidence of QMJC which were ignored in well-established theories of Josephson junctions.

</details>


### [126] [Excitron-Induced Pair Fluctuations Reveal Superconductivity in the Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08498)
*Yasutami Takada*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在低密度三维电子气中，通过第一性原理计算发现rs≈8、T~10^{-4}E_F处存在由准一维激子涨落驱动的二阶超导相变，揭示了等离激元介导超导通过激子通道实现的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超导电性如何在稀薄电子体系中涌现是凝聚态物理的核心挑战之一。本文旨在探索低密度三维电子气中超导转变的微观机制及其与准一维涨落的关系。

Method: 对低密度三维电子气进行第一性原理计算，求解电子自能Σ(k,iω_n)，分析其临界行为及激子涨落动力学。

Result: 在rs≈8、T~10^{-4}E_F处发现自能的尖锐发散，标志着二阶相变；该临界行为源于激子（电子与纵向电子-空穴对形成的准一维电子复合体）虚激发介导的一维超导涨落；在转变附近观察到赝能隙及线性温变的反向电子寿命。

Conclusion: 揭示了稀薄三维电子气中等离激元驱动超导电性通过准一维激子动力学实现的新路径，为理解高温超导材料中的类似现象提供了参考。

Abstract: Understanding how superconductivity can emerge in dilute electronic systems remains a central challenge in condensed matter physics. By performing first-principles calculations of the electron self-energy Σ(k,iw_n) in the low-density three-dimensional electron gas, we identify a sharp divergence at r_s ~ 8 and T ~ 10^{-4}E_ F, signaling a second-order phase transition. This critical behavior originates from one-dimensional superconducting fluctuations mediated by virtual excitations of an excitron---a quasi-1D electronic composite formed by an electron and longitudinal electron-hole pairs. Although the superconducting mechanism itself is plasmon-mediated, the excitron channel provides a unique window into its fluctuation dynamics. Near the transition, we observe a pseudogap and a linear-in-T inverse electron lifetime, reminiscent of phenomena in high-T_c materials. These results reveal an unexpected route by which plasmon-driven superconductivity manifests in the dilute 3D electron gas through quasi-1D excitron dynamics.

</details>


### [127] [Prediction of BaBiO$_3$-like superconducting perovskites in K-doped SrAsO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09020)
*Zhihong Yuan,Rui Liu,Pengyu Zheng,Zhiping Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 第一性原理计算预测了一种新型钙钛矿化合物SrAsO₃。未掺杂的立方相存在明显的软声子不稳定性，通过K掺杂Sr位可逐渐抑制这些不稳定性。当K掺杂水平超过约60%时，立方相变得动力学稳定，并表现出传统声子介导的超导电性。HSE06杂化泛函修正后，Sr₀.₄K₀.₆AsO₃的电子-声子耦合常数λ达到1.41，预测的Tc为44.3 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索新型钙钛矿超导体，特别是寻找类似于BaBiO₃的强电子-声子耦合驱动的超导材料，扩展钙钛矿超导家族。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）和密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）。使用HSE06杂化交换关联泛函修正非局域交换相互作用，计算电子结构、声子谱、电子-声子耦合及超导转变温度Tc。

Result: （1）未掺杂立方相SrAsO₃存在软声子不稳定性；（2）K掺杂超过60%可动力学稳定立方相；（3）稳定相呈金属性，具有传统声子介导超导电性；（4）HSE06杂化泛函拓宽了电子带宽，增强了EPC强度；（5）Sr₀.₄K₀.₆AsO₃的EPC常数λ达1.41，预测Tc为44.3 K。

Conclusion: SrAsO₃是一种类似于BaBiO₃的、由强电子-声子耦合驱动的超导钙钛矿材料，K掺杂是稳定其立方相并诱导超导电性的有效手段。

Abstract: Using first-principles calculations, we predict a new perovskite compound SrAsO$_3$ . The undoped cubic phase has pronounced soft-phonon instabilities, which are gradually suppressed upon K doping the Sr site. The cubic phase becomes dynamically stable for K-doping levels above approximately 60%, and the stabilized K-doped phases are metallic with predicted conventional phonon mediated superconductivity. Moreover, the inclusion of nonlocal exchange interactions broadens the electronic bandwidth, enhances the electron-phonon coupling (EPC) strength, and increases the superconducting transition temperature ($T_c$) of these doped compounds. In particular, the HSE06 hybrid exchange-correlation functional corrected EPC constant $λ$ reaches 1.41 for Sr$_{0.4}$K$_{0.6}$AsO$_3$, corresponding to a predicted $T_c$ of 44.3 K. These results suggest that SrAsO$_3$ is a BaBiO$_3$-like superconducting perovskite driven by strong electron-phonon coupling.

</details>


### [128] [Exchange-induced suppression of superconductivity in a nano-skyrmion lattice - superconductor hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09207)
*Dongfei Wang,Wenbin Li,Eric Mascot,Roland Wiesendanger*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在Nb(110)衬底上制备Fe/Ir(111)薄膜，实现了纳米斯格明子晶格与超导体的杂化体系。扫描隧道谱显示斯格明子晶格会抑制超导能隙和相干峰，紧束缚计算表明交换耦合增强会导致能隙逐渐填充直至超导淬灭，为设计拓扑非平庸态提供了微观约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非共线自旋织构（如斯格明子晶格）与超导体耦合以创造和调控非传统超导态，超越传统的铁磁/反铁磁-超导杂化体系。

Method: 在Nb(110)表面外延生长单层Fe on Ir(111)薄膜，构建纳米斯格明子晶格-超导杂化体系；利用扫描隧道谱（STS）进行实验表征；结合紧束缚模型进行理论计算，分析交换耦合强度对超导能隙的影响。

Result: 实验上，Fe纳米斯格明子晶格导致超导能隙和相干峰消失；理论上，紧束缚计算揭示随着交换耦合增强，超导能隙被逐步填充直至完全淬灭。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了磁-超导异质结中设计拓扑非平庸态的微观约束条件，表明斯格明子晶格与超导体的近邻耦合会抑制超导电性，需要精细调控交换耦合才能实现拓扑超导。

Abstract: Engineered magnet-superconductor hybrids have recently been identified as promising platforms for the investigation of topological superconductivity. Beyond ferro- and antiferromagnetic structures, coupling non-collinear spin textures, such as skyrmion lattices, to superconductors offers an exciting route for creating and manipulating unconventional superconducting states. In this work, by preparing monolayer Fe on Ir(111) thin films grown epitaxially on a Nb(110) surface, we realize a hybrid system of a nano-skyrmion lattice proximitized to a superconducting substrate. Scanning tunneling spectroscopy shows that superconductivity becomes suppressed by the Fe nano-skyrmion lattice, with both the superconducting gap and coherence peaks disappearing. Tight-binding calculations reveal that with increasing exchange coupling, the gap is progressively filled up and eventually superconductivity gets quenched. These results reveal microscopic constraints for designing topologically non-trivial states based on magnet-superconducting heterostructures.

</details>


### [129] [Revealing Hidden Unconventional Pairing through Nonreciprocal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09751)
*Wen-Bo Dai,Ming Gong,Xianxin Wu,Chui-Zhen Chen,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过多端器件中源-漏交换的非互易电导来识别超导体配对对称性的框架。该方法利用非互易输运对序参量对称性破缺分量的敏感性，通过特征角依赖关系编码配对间隙的动量空间结构：时间反演破缺的单态配对产生非互易电荷输运，而自旋三重态配对产生非互易自旋响应，为隐藏的非常规配对提供了对称性选择性的探测手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规超导体的配对对称性识别是理解其微观机制的核心步骤，但当非常规配对被主导的s波分量掩盖时，实验鉴别极为困难。需要发展一种能够绕过s波主导、对隐藏的非常规配对分量具有选择性敏感的新探测框架。

Method: 构建对称性分辨的理论框架：利用多端器件中交换源极与漏极产生的非互易电导；分析非互易输运如何由超导序参量的对称性破缺分量驱动，并推导其角依赖关系与配对间隙动量空间结构的对应关系；通过铁基超导体和非中心对称超导体的代表性模型进行理论验证，并设计多端测量的实验方案。

Result: （1）时间反演对称性破缺的单态配对导致非互易电荷输运；（2）自旋三重态配对产生非互易自旋响应；（3）非互易输运的角依赖关系直接编码配对间隙的动量空间结构，可作为区分不同配对对称性的输运指纹。

Conclusion: 该工作将非互易输运确立为一种对称性选择性探针，能够识别被s波分量掩盖的隐藏非常规配对，适用于广泛的超导材料体系，推进了超导体中非互易输运的理论理解。

Abstract: Identifying the pairing symmetry of Cooper pairs is a fundamental step toward understanding the microscopic mechanisms of unconventional superconductors. However, experimental identification remains a formidable challenge, particularly when unconventional pairing is obscured by a dominant $s$-wave component that masks its spectroscopic signatures. Here, we develop a symmetry-resolved framework to identify superconducting pairing symmetry through nonreciprocal conductance upon exchanging source and detector terminals in multiterminal devices. We show that nonreciprocal transport arises from symmetry-breaking components of the superconducting order parameter and exhibits a characteristic angular dependence that encodes the momentum-space structure of the pairing gap. In particular, time-reversal-breaking singlet pairing induces nonreciprocal charge transport, while spin-triplet pairing generates nonreciprocal spin responses, providing distinct transport fingerprints of the underlying order. We demonstrate this mechanism using representative models of iron-based and noncentrosymmetric superconductors and outline experimental protocols for multiterminal measurements. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of nonreciprocal transport in superconductors, and establish it as a symmetry-selective probe for identifying hidden unconventional pairing in a wide range of superconducting materials.

</details>


### [130] [Superconductivity, pseudogap and marginal Fermi liquid in a relative-momentum-local theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09785)
*Jian-Jian Miao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文构建了相对动量局域（RML）理论，以t-J模型中反铁磁超交换的精确自旋单态Cooper表示为主线，统一描述了铜氧化物超导体中的d波超导、赝能隙和奇异金属三种相，预言了零能Cooper面脊作为2e-ARPES的关键实验信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体中的d波超导、赝能隙（含费米弧）和奇异金属（线性电阻）三种相虽源于同一掺杂Mott系统，但通常被分别描述，缺乏一个统一的理论框架将其串联起来。

Method: 作者以t-J模型中反铁磁超交换的精确自旋单态Cooper表示为核心，构建相对动量局域（RML）理论。通过静态RML方案得到严格可解的赝能隙哈密顿量（具有节点电子极点、反节点能隙、费米弧和电荷-2e Cooper面）；通过Cooper面Lifshitz相变解释比热系数和电荷响应的对数增强；通过动态RML方案得到边缘费米液体标度的一圈自能；利用物理对谱的谱权重转移诊断赝能隙-奇异金属 crossover，用非零相位刚度和全局相位相干性标识超导。

Result: (1) 静态RML给出可解赝能隙模型：节点电子极点、反节点能隙、分辨展宽费米弧、无能隙电荷-2e Cooper面；(2) Cooper面Lifshitz相变产生比热和电荷响应的对数增强，无自旋奇异性；(3) 有限质心动量下的粒子-粒子连续谱产生欧姆阻尼；(4) 动态RML得到边缘费米液体标度自能；(5) 谱权重转移诊断赝能隙-奇异金属 crossover，相位刚度和全局相干性标识超导。

Conclusion: RML理论将单粒子、对敏感和热力学探针统一归结为同一Cooper通道的三种红外组织方式，为铜氧化物相图提供了统一描述。关键实验检验是双电子角分辨光电子能谱（2e-ARPES）中的零能Cooper面脊。

Abstract: Cuprate superconductors host \(d\)-wave superconductivity (SC), a pseudogap (PG) with Fermi arcs and a strange metal (SM) with incoherent excitations and temperature-linear resistivity. These regimes emerge from the same doped Mott system but are usually described separately. Here a relative-momentum-local (RML) theory is constructed, with the exact spin-singlet Cooper representation of antiferromagnetic superexchange in the \(t\)-\(J\) model serving as a common thread. Pair correlations organize the low-energy behaviour of this Cooper channel into phase-coherent static SC, phase-incoherent quasi-static PG and phase-incoherent dynamic SM. A static RML prescription yields an exactly solvable PG Hamiltonian with nodal electron poles, antinodal gaps, resolution-broadened Fermi arcs and gapless charge-\(2e\) Cooper surfaces. A Cooper-surface Lifshitz transition generates logarithmic enhancements in the specific-heat coefficient and charge response without a spin singularity. The particle-particle continuum at finite centre-of-mass momentum produces Ohmic damping, while a dynamic RML prescription yields a one-loop self-energy with marginal-Fermi-liquid scaling. Spectral-weight transfer in the physical pair spectrum diagnoses the PG--SM crossover, whereas nonzero phase stiffness and global phase coherence identify SC. The RML theory relates single-particle, pair-sensitive and thermodynamic probes to three infrared organizations of one Cooper channel. A decisive experimental signature would be zero-energy Cooper-surface ridges in two-electron angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (2e-ARPES).

</details>
